## 3. TEST EXECUTION

### Prerequisites

- Working directory is the repository root.
- The `runtime/` vitest suites are installed under `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime`.

### Prompt

- Prompt: `Check that a fan-out research run under expansive depth mode deals the seeded depth-one nodes round-robin across its lineages, writes each lineage store before spawn, and fails the merge guard when a partitioned node is missing. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.`

### Commands

1. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run fanout-run.vitest -t "round-robin" --reporter=verbose`
2. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run fanout-run.vitest -t "writes the lineage store before spawn" --reporter=verbose`
3. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run fanout-run.vitest -t "default depth mode" --reporter=verbose`
4. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run fanout-merge.vitest -t "partitioned node" --reporter=verbose`
5. `rg -n "frontier_scope|partitionFrontierNodes|writeLineageFrontierStore" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs`

### Expected

Command 1 prints `Tests  2 passed | 206 skipped (208)`, with the cases `deals open depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order` and `deals seven review frontier nodes over three seats round-robin` passing. Command 2 prints `Tests  1 passed | 207 skipped (208)`, with the case `writes the lineage store before spawn and records the partition` passing. Command 3 prints `Tests  1 passed | 207 skipped (208)`, with the case `a default depth mode writes no lineage store and records no partition` passing. Command 4 prints `Tests  4 passed | 86 skipped (90)`, with the cases `passes the guard when every partitioned node is reported` and `fails the guard when a partitioned node is missing from every lineage` passing under both the research and the review frontier merge. Command 5 prints the partition helper, the store writer and the `frontier_scope` binding.

### Evidence

- The verbose case lines and summary lines from commands 1 to 4, with each exit status.
- The `rg` lines from command 5, read from the current source.

### Pass / Fail

- **Pass**: All four commands exit 0 with the expected counts, and command 5 shows the partition and store helpers.
- **Fail**: A seeded node goes to no lineage, a store is written after spawn, a default run writes a store, or the merge guard passes a missing node.

### Failure Triage

- A seeded node goes to no lineage: check the round-robin deal over node-id order in the partition helper.
- A store appears after spawn: check that the store writer runs in the lineage worker before the prompt is built and spawned.
- The merge guard passes a missing node: check that the guard reads the partition recorded in the orchestration summary.

127:`--depth-mode=expansive` lets a run grow its own questions. 165:`/speckit:plan` and `/speckit:implement` consume the report. `/speckit:resume` rebuilds context before you extend a completed run. `/speckit:save` writes a manual memory save, though a research run already saves at the end.

const ROOT_CHILDREN_FLOOR = Object.freeze({ research: 6, review: 8 });

const NODE_STATUSES = Object.freeze([
  'open',
  'active',
  'answered',
  'blocked',
  'exhausted',
  'abstained',
  'pruned',
]);

// close cannot move a node back to the unopened or admitted state: seeding and
// admission own those, and a re-close is only meaningful for a lifecycle move.
const CLOSE_STATUSES = new Set([
  'active',
  'answered',
  'blocked',
  'exhausted',
  'abstained',
  'pruned',
]);

// A node closed with one of these never answered its question: the branch ran
// out, or it was dropped. The graph projection has to keep that visible.
const UNANSWERED_CLOSE_STATUSES = new Set([
  'blocked',
  'exhausted',
  'abstained',
]);

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 3. HELPERS
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function isPlainObject(value) {
  return Boolean(value) && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function isNonEmptyString(value) {
  return typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() !== '';
}

function hasScope(scope) {
  if (typeof scope === 'string') return scope.trim() !== '';
  return Array.isArray(scope) && scope.length > 0;
}

function normalizeIteration(value) {
  return Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 ? value : 0;
}

function capValue(value, fallback) {
  return Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0 ? Math.floor(value) : fallback;
}

function assertTarget(target) {
  if (!TARGETS.includes(target)) {
    throw new Error(`unknown target: ${String(target)}; expected ${TARGETS.join(' or ')}`);
  }
  return target;
}

function appendJsonlRow(filePath, row) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.appendFileSync(filePath, `${JSON.stringify(row)}\n`, 'utf8');
}

function nodeDedupeKey(node) {
  return typeof node.dedupeKey === 'string' ? node.dedupeKey : dedupeKey(node.question);
}

function depthFor(proposal, nodes) {
  const parentId = isPlainObject(proposal) ? proposal.parentId : null;
  if (parentId === 'root') return 1;
  const parent = nodes.get(parentId);
  // A node with no resolvable parent was never placed on the graph. Zero keeps
  // that fact visible instead of inventing a depth the node never had.
  return parent && Number.isFinite(parent.depth) ? parent.depth + 1 : 0;
}

Checkpoint commits provide rollback points: `git log -- research/` shows the last good state for the research packet. If state corruption occurs, `git checkout HEAD~1 -- research/deep-research-state.jsonl` restores the previous version.

#### Step 5: Loop Decision
- If convergence check returns STOP: exit to synthesis
- If STUCK_RECOVERY: modify focus directive, reset stuck count, continue
- Otherwise: increment iteration counter, go to Step 1

### Expansive Depth Frontier (opt-in)

Under `--depth-mode=expansive` the loop keeps a frontier of follow-up questions beside the key questions seeded at init. The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode: "expansive"` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 per depth, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to `maxIterations`. A run without the flag keeps the default path and its behavior. The one byte change is the expansive-only persona paragraph in the CLI lineage prompt, which the golden test strips before it compares.

`step_frontier_seed` decomposes the topic into 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1. On a plain run it sits in `phase_init` after `step_create_config`; on a fan-out run it runs before `step_fanout_spawn`. It calls `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed`.

A leaf may append `frontier_proposal` rows to its delta only when its prompt carries a FRONTIER block, which `frontier-admit.cjs block` computes from the node's remaining child slots and the prompt pack renders through `{frontier_block}`. `step_frontier_admit` runs before `step_reduce_state` and calls `frontier-admit.cjs admit`, which applies six checks in order: relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget, cycle. The first failure prunes the proposal with that reason. Admitted nodes become `research/inbox.jsonl` rows with `origin: "frontier"`.

Next Focus picks the shallowest open node first, then the least visited, then the oldest, and writes `Frontier node <id> (depth N, scope S): <question>` so a visit does not read as an answer.

Under stuck recovery the handler closes the focused node as `exhausted` with reason `stuck` through `frontier-admit.cjs close`, keeps the `stuckRecovery` event, and moves to the next open node. Global recovery runs only when no node is open.

On a fan-out run, `fanout-run.cjs --depth-mode expansive` deals the seeded open depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order across lineages, writes each lineage's `frontier-nodes.jsonl` and inbox rows before it spawns, binds `frontier_scope:` in the lineage prompt and records `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json`. `fanout-merge.cjs` merges the lineage frontiers, each node keeping its lineage label, and records a partition guard that names any dealt node no lineage reports.

### Ideas Backlog Convention

The canonical ideas backlog is reducer-owned and derived from JSONL lifecycle events. A persistent ideas file at `research/research-ideas.md` may still exist as operator-authored context, but leaf agents record new machine-discovered tangents with `idea_observed` events.

#### Check Points

The orchestrator checks the ideas backlog at three points:

1. **Init**: During strategy initialization, if `research/research-ideas.md` exists from a prior session, read it as operator-authored context and incorporate relevant ideas into the initial key questions or "Next Focus"
2. **Stuck**: During stuck recovery (Step 2a of the recovery protocol in convergence.md), check reducer-promoted ideas before defaulting to generic recovery strategies. Promoted ideas often provide the best escape from stuck states
3. **Resume**: On auto-resume, read JSONL, registry, strategy.md, and any operator-authored ideas file to restore full context

#### Event Format

```json
{"type":"event","event":"idea_observed","mode":"research","run":4,"ideaId":"idea-cache-stampede","idea":"Investigate cache stampede mitigation","category":"ideas","source":"iteration-004.md","timestamp":"2026-05-24T00:00:00Z"}
{"type":"event","event":"idea_promoted","mode":"research","run":5,"ideaId":"idea-cache-stampede","idea":"Investigate cache stampede mitigation","category":"ideas","observationCount":2,"minIdeaObservations":2,"firstObservedRun":4,"lastObservedRun":5,"timestamp":"2026-05-24T00:01:00Z"}
{"type":"event","event":"idea_rejected","mode":"research","run":6,"ideaId":"idea-cache-stampede","idea":"Investigate cache stampede mitigation","category":"ideas","reason":"Operator rejected this direction","timestamp":"2026-05-24T00:02:00Z"}

  return prompt;
}

function buildNativeCommandInput(loopType, specFolder, lineageDir, lineage, options = {}) {
  assertActiveFanoutLoopType(loopType);
  const maxIterations = lineage.iterations || 12;
  const convergenceThreshold = options.convergenceThreshold ?? (loopType === 'research' ? 0.05 : 0.1);
  const stopPolicy = options.stopPolicy || 'convergence';
  const sessionId = options.sessionId || '';
  const reviewTarget = loopType === 'review' && options.reviewTarget ? options.reviewTarget : specFolder;
  const reviewTargetType = loopType === 'review' && options.reviewTargetType
    ? options.reviewTargetType
    : 'spec-folder';
  const args = [
    ':auto',
    JSON.stringify(loopType === 'review' ? reviewTarget : specFolder),
    `--spec-folder=${specFolder}`,
    `--max-iterations=${maxIterations}`,
    `--convergence=${convergenceThreshold}`,
    `--stop-policy=${stopPolicy}`,
    ...(options.convergenceMode ? [`--convergence-mode=${options.convergenceMode}`] : []),
    ...(options.depthMode === 'expansive' ? ['--depth-mode=expansive'] : []),
    `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir=${lineageDir}`,
    '--lineage-mode=auto',
  ];
  if (loopType === 'research' && options.researchTopic) {
    args.push(`--research-topic=${JSON.stringify(options.researchTopic)}`);
  }
  const stopClause = hasIterationCap && stopPolicy === 'max-iterations'
    ? 'until config.maxIterations is reached; treat convergence before that as telemetry only and broaden review angles instead of synthesizing early'
    : hasIterationCap
      ? 'to legal convergence OR config.maxIterations, whichever comes first'
      : 'to legal convergence';
  const params = [
    `  spec_folder: ${specFolder}`,
    `  config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir: ${lineageDir}`,
    `  session_id: ${sessionId}`,
    `  executor: ${lineage.kind} model=${lineage.model || 'default'}`,
    `  loop_type: ${loopType}`,
    `  config.stopPolicy: ${stopPolicy}`,
  ];
  if (researchTopic) {
    // A research lineage's phase_init binds research_topic from this line. Without it a
    // fan-out research loop has no question to investigate and degrades to an empty topic.
    // Review loops scope by spec_folder and intentionally pass no topic.
    params.push(`  research_topic: ${researchTopic}`);
  }
  if (hasIterationCap) {
    params.push(`  config.maxIterations: ${lineage.iterations}`);
  }
  if (options.convergenceThreshold !== null && options.convergenceThreshold !== undefined) {
    params.push(`  config.convergenceThreshold: ${options.convergenceThreshold}`);
  }
  if (options.convergenceMode) {
    params.push(`  config.convergenceMode: ${options.convergenceMode}`);
  }
  if (options.depthMode === 'expansive') {
    params.push(`  config.depthMode: expansive`);
    params.push(`  frontier_scope: ${(options.frontierScope || []).join(', ') || 'none'}`);

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '230,260p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/SKILL.md
The stuck count and the consecutive failure count are rebuilt from the state log on every pass, so a resumed run counts the same as one that never paused. Failed iterations stay out of the ratio signals. Three trailing failed iterations stop the loop with stop reason `error`. In confirm mode only, the workflow first asks the operator Retry, Skip or Cancel. Auto mode has no such question.

Each pass also compares `maxDurationMinutes` with the config `createdAt`. When the limit is reached the loop skips the dispatch and goes to synthesis with stop reason `maxDurationReached`.

### Expansive Depth Mode

- `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a depth-capped frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. An absent flag means `default`, which runs exactly as before.
- The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, set to `maxIterations` at config creation. `antiConvergence.depthMode` is locked against the optimizer; the `expansiveDepth` caps are in neither its tunable nor its locked list.
- `step_frontier_seed` writes the depth-1 nodes before iteration 1 through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed --target review`, after `step_create_config` or before `step_fanout_spawn` on a fan-out run. A merge with no diff of its own is pruned `merge-no-own-diff` and an index rebuild `generated-artifact`.
- `step_frontier_admit` runs before the reducer and prunes a `frontier_proposal` on the first failed check among relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, written only by `frontier-admit.cjs`; review has no inbox.
- `step_read_state` reads the active node and sets `next_dimension` to its next uncovered dimension, `step_frontier_activate` marks it active before dispatch, and the prompt pack's `{frontier_block}` names the node. Next Focus takes the blocked stop, then the pivot, then the node, then the leaf focus, then the first uncovered dimension.
- While a node stays open the frontier veto turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the review floor, and stuck recovery closes the active node `exhausted`.
- A fan-out run deals the depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order, binds `frontier_scope:` beside `review_focus:`, records the partition as `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json` and returns a partition guard for any node no lineage reports. Size each lineage's iteration cap (the `--iters=N` value on its executor entry, which `fanout-run.cjs` passes as that lineage's `--max-iterations`, 12 when unset) so half of it covers `ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions`.

---

## 4. RULES

### ✅ ALWAYS

1. Read JSONL and strategy before review action.
2. Review one dimension per iteration and write findings to `iteration-NNN.md`.
3. Append JSONL with severity counts, finding detail, and `newInfoRatio`.
4. Cite every finding with `[SOURCE: file:line]`. Reject inference-only findings.
5. Re-read cited code before recording any P0.
6. Keep target files read-only.
7. Use `generate-context.js` for continuity saves. **Owner**: the workflow (`deep-review.yaml`) calls it at the save phase, not the reducer (`runtime/scripts/reduce-state.cjs`) directly — don't expect continuity-save side effects from the reducer alone.
8. Emit setup `BINDING:` lines before workflow output.
9. Refuse nested dispatch with: `REFUSE: nested Task tool dispatch is forbidden for LEAF agents. Returning partial findings instead.`

 ### Expansive Depth Mode

--L7-5--
**Key defaults:** `maxIterations=7`, `convergenceThreshold=0.10`, `rollingStopThreshold=0.08`, `noProgressThreshold=0.05`, `stuckThreshold=2`, `minIterations=2` (review floor, `antiConvergence.minIterations`), `minStabilizationPasses=1`
--L8-2--
Each pass runs in a fresh context against state kept on disk. A script, not the agent, decides when to stop. A review stays open below its minimum iteration count, while a P0 is active or while a required check still fails, and it ends in a PASS, CONDITIONAL or FAIL verdict. A loop survives crashes and new sessions, because its progress lives in files.

Two opt-in flags let a loop reach further. `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a frontier of sub-questions or sub-targets and turns a stop into another pass while one stays open, though never past the iteration cap. `--extend=N` reopens a finished single-executor run in the same session with room for N more passes, and `--extend-minutes=M` adds time. Under the default stop policy it can re-stop at once, so pair it with `--convergence-mode=off` when the run must keep going. A review can also track custom dimensions beside its four defaults.

A loop can also spread across several AI CLIs at once. Each executor runs its own lineage, and for a review the strictest verdict wins the merge. A pool caps how many lineages run at once. A stall watchdog stops a lineage that stops making progress, and a cost cap bounds what each one spends. The [Deep Loop README](.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/README.md) describes both modes and the shared runtime.

16:`--depth-mode` takes `default` or `expansive`. A review without the flag is unchanged. `expansive` seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows under the same caps as research, keeps node status in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, and holds open nodes as `searchDebt`. One live review pilot ran. Its depth-1 budget check passed, but its stop criterion failed: the root fan-out cap of 6, since raised to 8 for a review seed, pruned a seventh depth-1 node, and three of six lineages stopped on `maxIterationsReached`. This page describes the shipped code and its tests.

== L4-1 ==
## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Seeding And Admission

The expansive run seeds depth-one nodes before iteration 1. Each iteration's leaf `frontier_proposal` rows go through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs`, which admits each proposal into the node store. It applies the relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle checks in that order, and records the name of the first failing check as the reason for each prune.

### Configuration

`expansiveDepth` in `deep-research-config.json` sets `maxDepth` to 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` to 6, and a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths. `antiConvergence.depthMode` defaults to `default`. The convergence script adds `depthMode` to its stop-decision config only when the value is `expansive`.

### Stop And Next Focus

While a frontier node is open, a stop candidate, whether `all_questions_answered` or a composite convergence stop, becomes another iteration. Next Focus steers to the shallowest open node. The iteration cap, the anti-convergence floor, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` and the max-iterations policy decide before the frontier veto, so the frontier never extends a run past them.

### Fan-Out

Fan-out lineages each receive a share of the seeded nodes. `runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` passes `depthMode` to a lineage only when it is `expansive`. `runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` merges the lineage frontiers and runs a fan-in guard over the recorded partition.
== L4-3 ==

- `id`: stable inbox record identifier
- `text`: question text to promote
- `source`: concrete source label, such as an angle bank entry, analyst strategy, or operator note
- `origin`: one of `angle-bank`, `analyst-strategy`, `frontier`, `operator`, or `legacy-import`
- `injectedAtIteration`: iteration number when the question was introduced
- `promotedQuestionId`: promoted registry question id, or `null` until promotion

The reducer reads the inbox on every reduce step and carries `origin` into the question registry and dashboard badges. Direct edits to Section 3 still work as a compatibility path, but they are attributed as `legacy-import`.

== L3-3 block ==
`:confirm` path stays unchanged — see the Consolidated Setup Prompt section below.

### PRE-BOUND SETUP ANSWERS Schema (for `:auto` non-interactive dispatch)

The dispatched prompt body may contain one structured marker block. Parse it before applying defaults.

```yaml
PRE-BOUND SETUP ANSWERS:
  review_target: <spec-folder>
  review_target_type: spec-folder  # one of: spec-folder | skill | agent | track | files
  review_dimensions: all  # or comma-separated list: any of correctness, security, traceability, maintainability, plus custom names that follow them
  review_focus: ""  # optional free text every iteration covers in addition to its dimension
  spec_folder: existing  # one of: existing | new | update-related | phase-folder, or an explicit specs/.opencode/specs path
  execution_mode: AUTONOMOUS  # from :auto suffix
 lineage_mode: auto  # one of: auto | resume | restart | completed-continue (set by --extend=N)
 extend: ""  # optional positive integer N from --extend=N: extends a completed run by N iterations
 extend_minutes: ""  # optional positive integer M from --extend-minutes=M: minutes added to the duration budget, default the prior budget
  maxIterations: 10
  convergenceThreshold: 0.10
  convergence_mode: default  # default | off | sliding-window | divergent

### `:auto` Setup Resolution

Setup contract: see `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/references/workflows/auto-mode-contract.md`.

Under `execution_mode = AUTONOMOUS` (from the `:auto` suffix), follow the three-tier flow:

1. **Tier 1 — Resolve confidently** (contract §1): parse `$ARGUMENTS` flags + `PRE-BOUND SETUP ANSWERS:` block (§2) + the Default Resolution Table below (§3). When every required field is resolved, resolve `{artifact_dir}` and persist to `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-config.json` (shape: `reviewTarget`, `reviewTargetType`, `reviewDimensions`, `reviewFocus`, `specFolder`, `maxIterations`, `convergenceThreshold`, `antiConvergence.convergenceMode`, reserved `antiConvergence.divergent: {}`, `stopPolicy`, `executionMode: "auto"`, `lineageMode`, `resource_map.emit`, `config.executor.*`), bind runtime YAML placeholders, set `STATUS: PASSED`, load `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml` with `execution_mode` bound to `auto`. End §0.

2. **Tier 2 — Targeted ask** (contract §1): when 1-2 required fields are genuinely ambiguous AND no default exists, emit ONE narrow question per ambiguous field. Command-specific Tier-2-eligible fields (per the Default Resolution Table below): `review_target_type`, `spec_folder`. **Ordering rule**: if `review_target_type` is ambiguous, ask only for `review_target_type` first — the answer may make `spec_folder` self-evident on the next Tier 1 pass. Missing `review_target` is absence, not ambiguity — go to Tier 3.

3. **Tier 3 — Fail fast** (contract §4): emit the named-missing-inputs error format with `/deep:review:auto` as the command name. Exit non-zero. Do not load YAML.

| `fanout_concurrency` | N | flag `--concurrency=N` | `2` | N |

**Fan-out default policy:** 0–1 `--executor` flags and no `--executors`, or an `--executors` matrix that expands to exactly one lineage → `config.executor` (single-executor, default, unchanged). 2+ `--executor` flags, an `--executors` matrix that expands to two or more lineages, or any `--count > 1` → `config.fanout`. Review fan-out uses strongest-restriction: any lineage active P0 → merged FAIL. Native and CLI fan-out lineages run under `fanout-run.cjs` in the capped pool so every lineage shares retries, artifact checks, and stop-policy validation. The native adapter calls the `deep/review` command with the internal `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir` override; it must not dispatch the LEAF `@deep-review` agent or a pasted phase-running prompt as a full-loop sub-agent. When fan-out includes `cli-opencode`, that lineage is an explicit parallel-detached OpenCode session with its own lineage directory and session id; this is the documented exception to the cli-opencode self-invocation guard. Single-executor `cli-opencode` remains cross-runtime only.

### Key Features

#### Review Dimensions
- Iterative (multi-pass) code/spec audit across configurable dimensions
- Dimension ordering follows risk priority (correctness and security first)
- Each dimension can be individually included or excluded

#### Severity and Verdicts
- Three severity levels: P0 (Blocker), P1 (Required), P2 (Suggestion)
- Three verdicts: PASS, CONDITIONAL, FAIL
- PASS may include `hasAdvisories=true` when P2 findings exist
- P0 override: new P0 findings block convergence regardless of threshold

#### Convergence Detection

### Review Dimensions

The four primary review dimensions (configured in `assets/review-mode-contract.yaml`; configure them by the id in parentheses, because any other name counts as a custom dimension):

| Dimension | Focus | Key Questions |
|-----------|-------|---------------|
| **Correctness** (`correctness`) | Logic, behavior, error handling | Behavior matches claims? Edge cases handled? |
| **Security** (`security`) | Vulnerabilities, exposure, trust boundaries | Inputs validated? Credentials exposed? |
| **Spec-Alignment / Traceability** (`traceability`) | Spec vs. implementation fidelity | Code matches spec.md? Planned items present? |
| **Completeness / Maintainability** (`maintainability`) | Coverage, dead code, documentation | TODOs resolved? Code self-documenting? |

### Lifecycle + Reducer Contract

Review mode is lineage-aware.

- **Lifecycle modes:** `new`, `resume`, `restart`, and `completed-continue` (set by `--extend=N` on a completed run).
- **Required lineage fields:** `sessionId`, `parentSessionId`, `lineageMode`, `generation`, `continuedFromRun`, and `releaseReadinessState`.
- **Reducer contract:** consumes the latest JSONL delta, the new iteration file, and prior reduced state, then emits finding registry, dashboard metrics, and strategy updates.
== customDimensionRule ==

  # A configured name outside the four above is a custom dimension. It is tracked
  # and covered like the others, ordered after them, and judged by its own name.
  customDimensionRule:
    priority: after-canonical
    order: configured
    checks: Review the target for the property the configured name states, under the shared P0/P1/P2 severity doctrine.
    requiredForSeverityCoverage: false

### Review Dimensions

| Dimension | Use It For |
|-----------|------------|
| **Correctness** | Logic, state transitions, invariants, edge cases, and behavior against observable intent |
| **Security** | Trust boundaries, auth/authz, input handling, secrets exposure, and exploit paths |
| **Traceability** | Spec alignment, checklist evidence, cross-reference integrity, runtime parity, and named integration touchpoint coverage |
| **Maintainability** | Pattern compliance, documentation quality, clarity, and safe follow-on change cost |
| **Custom (configured)** | A configured custom dimension such as `performance`: the property its name states, judged under the shared P0/P1/P2 doctrine. It follows the canonical four and is not required for severity coverage |

### Binary Quality Gates

| Gate | Pass Condition |
|------|----------------|
| **Evidence** | Every active finding has concrete `file:line` evidence; active P0/P1 findings never rely only on inference |
| **Scope** | Findings stay inside the declared review target and review boundaries |
| **Coverage** | Required dimensions and traceability protocols are covered before STOP |
| **State Integrity** | Iteration artifact, strategy, and JSONL all describe the same run, focus, counts, status, and edge cases |
| **Integration Naming** | External routing or traceability claims name the exact command, workflow, skill, MCP/code tool, caller agent, or mirror path involved |

### Verdicts

| Verdict | Condition | Follow-on |
|---------|-----------|-----------|
| **FAIL** | Active `P0` exists. Whether a failed binary gate also forces FAIL is the rule of the session verdict the workflow derives at synthesis | `/speckit:plan` |
| **CONDITIONAL** | No active `P0`, but active `P1` remains | `/speckit:plan` |
| **PASS** | No active `P0` or `P1`; set `hasAdvisories=true` when active `P2` remains | `/create:changelog` |

### Iteration Final-Line Contract (MANDATORY)

// The canonical four come first in risk order; configured custom names follow in configured order.
function buildDimensionUniverse(config) {
  const universe = [...REQUIRED_DIMENSIONS];
  const raw = config && config.reviewDimensions;
  const configured = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : (typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split(',') : []);
  for (const entry of configured) {
    const name = String(entry).trim().toLowerCase();
    if (name && name !== 'all' && !universe.includes(name)) {
      universe.push(name);
    }
  }
  return universe;
}

function deriveDimension(finding, iteration, universe = REQUIRED_DIMENSIONS) {
  const focus = `${iteration.focus} ${finding.title} ${finding.description}`.toLowerCase();
  for (const dimension of universe) {
    if (focus.includes(dimension)) {
      return dimension;
    }
  }
  return iteration.dimensionsAddressed[0] || 'correctness';
}

// A node is answered once every dimension in the review's universe has evidence
// behind it. That status is derived on every reduce instead of written back, so
// the store keeps only the lifecycle its writer owns and the projection stays a
// pure function of the rows it read.
function buildReviewFrontier(storeData, iterationRecords, config) {
  const universe = buildDimensionUniverse(config);
  const history = Array.isArray(storeData?.history) ? storeData.history : [];
  const storedNodes = Array.isArray(storeData?.nodes) ? storeData.nodes : [];
  const nodes = storedNodes.map((node) => {
    const nodeId = node.nodeId;
    const records = reviewRecordsForNode(nodeId, history, iterationRecords)
      .filter((record) => !FRONTIER_FAILED_STATUSES.has(String(record.status || '').toLowerCase()));
    const covered = new Set();
    for (const record of records) {
      if (!Array.isArray(record.dimensions)) continue;
      for (const dimension of record.dimensions) {
        const name = String(dimension).trim().toLowerCase();
        if (name) covered.add(name);
      }
    }
    const coveredDimensions = universe.filter((dimension) => covered.has(dimension));
    const stillOpen = node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active';
    const status = stillOpen && coveredDimensions.length === universe.length ? 'answered' : node.status;
    return {
      nodeId,
      parentId: node.parentId ?? null,
      depth: node.depth,
      scope: node.scope ?? null,
      question: node.question ?? null,
      status,
      reason: status === node.status ? (node.reason ?? null) : null,
      iteration: node.iteration ?? null,
      coveredDimensions,
      nextDimension: universe.find((dimension) => !covered.has(dimension)) ?? null,
      visits: records.length,
    };
  });

      step_check_convergence:
        action: "Branch on the stop decision that convergence.cjs computed in step_graph_convergence"
        algorithm: |
          The whole stop decision is computed by convergence.cjs from the loop config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. This step performs no arithmetic and never recomputes or overrides the result.
          The ratio signals leave out every iteration whose status is thought, error or timeout, and stuck_count is loop_counters_json.stuckCount.
          The script applies this fixed order, first match wins:
          1. if iteration_count >= max_iterations: STOP (maxIterationsReached). This terminal cap bypasses every guard and veto; failed guards are recorded in stop_decision.blockedBy as synthesis evidence.
          1a. After 3 consecutive failed iterations (status error or timeout), halt the loop and enter synthesis with stop reason error and partial findings.
          2. if stuck_count >= config.stuckThreshold: STUCK_RECOVERY
          3. if convergence_mode == "off": skip convergence stop candidates and continue.
          3a. if stop_policy == "max-iterations" AND iteration_count < max_iterations: the vote is telemetry only and the decision is CONTINUE.
          4. a STOP candidate exists when every question is answered (all_questions_answered) or the weighted vote passes (composite_converged).
          5. if iteration_count < effective_min_iterations: CONTINUE (minIterationsNotReached)
          6. a failed quality guard turns the candidate into CONTINUE (qualityGuardFailed)
          7. the graph decision settles the candidate: STOP_ALLOWED keeps STOP, STOP_BLOCKED gives BLOCKED, CONTINUE or a missing graph decision gives CONTINUE.
          Bind the outputs from stop_decision:
            decision = stop_decision.decision
            reason = stop_decision.stopReason when it is not null, else stop_decision.reasonDetail
            reason_detail = stop_decision.reasonDetail
            weighted_stop_score = stop_decision.weightedStopScore
            stop_blocked = (stop_decision.decision == "BLOCKED")
            blocked_by_json = stop_decision.blockedBy
            blocked_by_csv = the members of stop_decision.blockedBy joined with commas
            gate_convergence_pass = (stop_decision.graphDecision == "STOP_ALLOWED")
            gate_convergence_score = graph_convergence_score
            gate_key_question_coverage_pass = no blocker named questionCoverage
            gate_evidence_density_pass = no blocker named sourceDiversity or evidenceDepth
            gate_evidence_density_sources = graph_signals_json.sourceDiversity or 0
            gate_hotspot_saturation_pass = true
            recovery_strategy = first blocker reason/message, else "Collect deeper graph evidence before stopping."
            min_iterations_guard_blocked = (stop_decision.reasonDetail == "minIterationsNotReached")
            min_iterations_guard_pass = true when effective_min_iterations > 1 and iteration_count >= effective_min_iterations and min_iterations_guard_already_passed == false
          If stop_decision is missing from the step_graph_convergence output, do not decide by hand: halt and report the script output.
          When stop_decision.reasonDetail == "qualityGuardFailed", log: "Quality guard failed: {blocked_by_csv}. Continuing."
          Segment-aware convergence is experimental/reference-only; live workflow uses the current session as a single segment.
        outputs:
          - decision: "STOP | BLOCKED | STUCK_RECOVERY | CONTINUE"
          - reason: "stop_decision.stopReason when set, else stop_decision.reasonDetail"
          - reason_detail: "stop_decision.reasonDetail, the fine cause behind the decision"
          - weighted_stop_score: "stop_decision.weightedStopScore, for diagnostics only"
          - stop_blocked: "Boolean true when stop_decision.decision == BLOCKED"
          - blocked_by_json: "JSON array of graph blocker names when decision == BLOCKED"
          - blocked_by_csv: "Comma-separated graph blocker names when decision == BLOCKED"
          - gate_convergence_pass: "Boolean pass/fail for the graph-assisted convergence gate"
          - gate_convergence_score: "Numeric score recorded from graph convergence"
          - gate_key_question_coverage_pass: "Boolean pass/fail derived from graph blocker coverage signals"
          - gate_evidence_density_pass: "Boolean pass/fail derived from graph blocker evidence-depth signals"

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '80,105p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/SKILL.md
### Stop Decision And Counters

- The stop decision is computed once per loop pass by `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` from the packet files. A missing graph decision blocks every non-terminal STOP.
- The stuck count and the consecutive failure count are derived from the state log on every pass, so a resumed run counts the same as one that never paused. Failed iterations stay out of the ratio signals.
- Three trailing failed iterations stop the loop with stop reason `error`. In confirm mode the workflow first asks Retry, Skip or Cancel. Auto mode has no such question.
- Each pass compares `maxDurationMinutes` with the config `createdAt`. At the limit the loop skips the dispatch and goes to synthesis with stop reason `maxDurationReached`.
- `deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` is the research reducer and the only writer of the dashboard.

### Expansive Depth Mode

- `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a depth-capped frontier of follow-up questions beside the seeded key questions. An absent flag means `default`, which keeps the default behavior. The one byte change is the expansive-only persona paragraph in the CLI lineage prompt, which the golden test strips before it compares.
- `step_frontier_seed` writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1 through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed`, after `step_create_config`, or before `step_fanout_spawn` on a fan-out run.
- `step_frontier_admit` runs before `step_reduce_state` and prunes a `frontier_proposal` on the first failed check among relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle. Admitted nodes land in `research/inbox.jsonl` with `origin: "frontier"`.
- Next Focus reads the shallowest open node first, then the least visited, then the oldest, and writes `Frontier node <id> (depth N, scope S): <question>`.
- After the `minIterations` floor an open or active node turns a soft stop into CONTINUE with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never creates a stop and never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, `--convergence-mode=off` or `--stop-policy=max-iterations`.
- A fan-out run deals the seeded depth-1 nodes round-robin across lineages and merges them back under a partition guard, so no dealt node goes unreported.

### Invocation Contract

This skill is invoked exclusively through `/deep:research:auto` or `/deep:research:confirm` -- the command YAML owns state, dispatch, convergence, and synthesis. Never simulate the loop with ad hoc shell dispatch, nested CLI loops, direct `@deep-research` Task dispatch, `/tmp` prompt files, or state outside the resolved local research packet.

### Executor Selection Contract

The YAML workflow owns executor selection: native `@deep-research` by default, or a routed CLI executor. Never use ad hoc shell loops.
Cross-CLI delegation inside an executor sandbox is possible but discouraged.

function graphHotspotSaturation(graph) {
  if (!isPlainObject(graph) || graph.failed === true) return null;
  const trace = Array.isArray(graph.trace) ? graph.trace : [];
  const entry = trace.find(
    (candidate) => isPlainObject(candidate) && candidate.signal === 'hotspotSaturation',
  );
  return entry !== undefined && typeof entry.passed === 'boolean' ? entry.passed : null;
}

---
title: "Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)"
description: "Opt-in --depth-mode=expansive on deep-review seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target, keeps node status in review/frontier-nodes.jsonl, and holds a review stop while nodes stay open."
trigger_phrases:
  - "--depth-mode=expansive review"
  - "expansive depth review"
  - "review frontier mode"
  - "review frontier-nodes"
version: 1.0.1.0
---

# Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)

## 1. OVERVIEW

`--depth-mode` takes `default` or `expansive`. A review without the flag is unchanged. `expansive` seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows under the same caps as research, keeps node status in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, and holds open nodes as `searchDebt`. One live review pilot ran. Its depth-1 budget check passed, but its stop criterion failed: the root fan-out cap of 6, since raised to 8 for a review seed, pruned a seventh depth-1 node, and three of six lineages stopped on `maxIterationsReached`. This page describes the shipped code and its tests.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Seeding And Admission

The expansive run seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1. Leaf `frontier_proposal` rows are admitted under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps. Merge commits are pruned as `merge-no-own-diff`, and index rebuilds are pruned as `generated-artifact`.

### Dimension Coverage

The active node's next uncovered dimension is dispatched. A node needs one iteration per dimension in its universe, the four canonical dimensions plus any custom name. Each depth band starts from its `budgetSplit` share of `--max-iterations` (0.5, 0.35 and 0.15), plus any share an earlier band left unspent once that band holds no open node, so fund the depth-1 band with at least `2 * ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions`.

### Stop Behavior

A review stop candidate becomes another iteration while nodes stay open. The `frontierOpen` veto applies to the review stop candidates, including `all_dimensions_clean` and the composite stop, after the minIterations floor.

### Pivot

The divergent review pivot runs only once the frontier is terminal. It seeds from terminal unanswered nodes and routes accepted candidates through admission.

### Fan-Out

Fan-out lineages receive a share of the seeded nodes. `runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` returns the merged review frontier and runs the review fan-in guard over the recorded partition.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/commands/deep/review.md` | Command doc | The `--depth-mode` flag row. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml` | Command workflow | The `depth_mode` input, the frontier seed step and the config keys. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/assets/deep-review-config.json` | Config | `antiConvergence.depthMode`, the `expansiveDepth` block and the locked `depthMode` field. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs` | Script | Admits review proposals as node-store rows; the reducer files the nodes it leaves open as `searchDebt`. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` | Script | Reads the review frontier, the close rule and the per-node dimension cover. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` | Script | Applies the frontier-open veto to review stop candidates. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/scripts/divergent-review-pivot.ts` | Script | Pivots only once the frontier is terminal. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` | Script | Returns the merged review frontier and runs the fan-in guard. |

title: "DRV-074 -- Configured custom dimension joins the review dimension set"

# DRV-074 -- Configured custom dimension joins the review dimension set

This document captures the realistic user-testing contract, current behavior, execution flow, source anchors, and metadata for `DRV-074`.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This scenario validates how a custom review dimension enters a review run at setup for `DRV-074`. It focuses on three steps: the setup rule normalizes the configured names, the dimension universe lists the four canonical dimensions first and each custom name after them, and the review prompt names every configured dimension in that order.

### Why This Matters

A review that names only the four canonical dimensions cannot check a property such as performance. A custom name that the setup rule rejects, drops, or places in the wrong order would leave the run checking a different set of dimensions than the operator asked for, and the review prompt would not tell the reviewer about the extra dimension.

...
15:This scenario validates how a custom review dimension enters a review run at setup for `DRV-074`. It focuses on three steps: the setup rule normalizes the configured names, the dimension universe lists the four canonical dimensions first and each custom name after them, and the review prompt names every configured dimension in that order.
25:Operators run this as a setup check. They read the rule that accepts the custom name, the universe that the reducer builds from it and the prompt line that the reviewer receives. No live review iteration is needed.
30:- Expected execution process: Read the review_dimensions input text and the normalize rule in the deep-review workflow, then read the dimension universe builder in the reducer, then run the reducer and prompt-pack cases that name a custom dimension.
31:- Expected signals: The workflow input names custom names as allowed, and the normalize rule trims, lowercases and drops duplicates, and fails an empty name. The universe builder returns the four canonical names followed by each configured custom name. The prompt-pack case renders the dimension line as `correctness, security, traceability, maintainability, performance`.
33:- Pass/fail: PASS if the setup rule accepts `performance`, the universe lists it after the four canonical names, and the reducer and prompt-pack cases pass; FAIL if a custom name is rejected or dropped, if it appears before a canonical name, or if the prompt line omits it.
60:- **Pass**: The setup rule accepts a custom name and normalizes it, the universe builder places it after the four canonical names, and command 3 and command 4 exit 0 with the named cases passing.
61:- **Fail**: The setup rule rejects or drops the custom name, the universe places it before a canonical name, the prompt line omits it, or a named case fails.

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '155,162p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/README.md
Any new P0 forces another iteration. The override sets `newFindingsRatio` to at least 0.50, so a fresh blocker cannot be averaged away. The shipped config adds `antiConvergence.minIterations` of 2 and a `stopPolicy` of `fail-closed`, and the runtime capability loader rejects a missing or permissive stop policy. The floor holds a review run at `CONTINUE` with reason `minIterationsNotReached` until its iteration count reaches `minIterations`, even when every dimension is clean.

`--depth-mode=expansive` is the one stop-path change that grows work instead of ending it. A seed step writes the depth-1 sub-target nodes before iteration 1, the active node's next uncovered dimension steers the iteration, and an admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, pruning the proposal on the first failure. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, which no default run creates, and an open node is filed as open search debt, so the run spends the iteration that node still needs. The frontier veto never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the floor above, and stuck recovery closes the active node as `exhausted`. The caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.

### Fan-out and Merged Verdicts

One run can spread the loop across executor lineages. Pass `--executor` once per lineage, or pass an `--executors` matrix. Cap the pool with `--concurrency`. The merged verdict is strongest restriction: any lineage with an active P0, or a required gate still failing at its stop, turns the merged verdict into FAIL.
> build · deepseek-v4.1-flash
[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '168,195p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/convergence/convergence.md
- `graphBlockerDetail`: array of structured graph blockers copied from the latest graph convergence decision. Empty when graph convergence did not veto STOP.
- `recoveryStrategy`: human-readable one-liner describing what the next iteration should do before another stop attempt.
- When the graph convergence verdict is `STOP_BLOCKED`, fold the graph blocker gate name into `blockedBy` and preserve the structured blocker objects under `graphBlockerDetail`.

### Decision Priority

Checks are evaluated in this order (first match wins), as `buildStopDecision` in `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` runs them:

1. **Max iterations** -- hard cap, always checked first. It outranks every veto below.
2. **Consecutive failures** -- three trailing failed iterations stop the run with stop reason `error`.
3. **Stuck detection** -- `stuckThreshold` (2) consecutive no-progress iterations route to stuck recovery.
4. **Convergence mode off** -- `convergenceMode: off` returns `CONTINUE` (`convergenceModeOff`).
5. **Stop policy max-iterations** -- `stopPolicy: max-iterations` returns `CONTINUE` (`stopPolicyMaxIterations`).
6. **Stop candidate** -- all dimensions covered and clean (every configured dimension covered, the four by default, no active P0/P1, coverage stabilized) is a hard candidate; otherwise the 3-signal composite vote with threshold 0.60 is a soft one. No candidate returns `CONTINUE` (`noStopCandidate`).
7. **Iteration floor** -- below `minIterations` the candidate returns `CONTINUE` (`minIterationsNotReached`).
8. **Frontier veto** -- under `--depth-mode=expansive`, an open frontier node turns the candidate into `CONTINUE` (`frontierOpen`).
9. **Legal-stop gate bundle** -- review-specific stop gates. If the candidate fails any gate, persist `stopReason=blockedStop` and continue.
10. **Graph decision** -- the candidate is combined with the graph convergence verdict: `STOP_ALLOWED` stops, `STOP_BLOCKED` blocks the stop and `CONTINUE` continues the run.

# DRV-081 -- Review pivot waits for a terminal frontier

This document captures the realistic user-testing contract, current behavior, execution flow, source anchors, and metadata for `DRV-081`.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This scenario validates the review divergent pivot gate for `DRV-081`. It focuses on the refusal while a frontier node is open, on the revisit directions the pivot seeds from sub-targets the frontier closed without covering every dimension, and on the default run, which keeps the pivot exactly as before.

### Why This Matters

The pivot reopens the review from a new angle. If it ran while a sub-target was still open, it would compete with the frontier for the same ground. If it ignored the sub-targets the frontier gave up on, those sub-targets would never get a second look.

---

## 2. SCENARIO CONTRACT

Operators run the commands below and confirm that the pivot waits for a terminal frontier, seeds a revisit for each closed sub-target it left uncovered, and leaves the default run alone.

- Objective: Confirm that the review pivot refuses to run while a frontier node is open, that it seeds one `frontier-unanswered` direction per closed sub-target the frontier left uncovered, and that a default run seeds no frontier direction even when its registry carries an open node.
- Real user request: Why did the review skip its divergent pivot, and does it still check the sub-targets it gave up on?
- Prompt: `Check that the deep-review divergent pivot refuses to run while a frontier node is open, seeds one revisit direction for each closed sub-target the frontier left uncovered, and leaves a default run untouched. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.`
- Expected execution process: Run the review pivot cases by file stem, run the workflow case that gates the pivot on a terminal frontier, then read the line in the pivot script that labels a seed `frontier-unanswered`.
- Expected signals: The three pivot cases pass. The refusal case throws for an open node. The seed case creates one direction for the exhausted sub-target and none for the answered one. The default-run case keeps no frontier direction. The workflow gate case passes, and the pivot script labels the seed `frontier-unanswered`.
- Desired user-visible outcome: The operator can say the pivot waited until the frontier closed, and each sub-target the frontier gave up on got one revisit direction.
- Pass/fail: PASS if the pivot cases, the gate case and the seed label all pass or print; FAIL if the pivot runs while a node is open, an answered sub-target gets a revisit, or a default run seeds a frontier direction.

---

## 3. TEST EXECUTION

### Prerequisites

- Working directory is the repository root.
- The `runtime/` vitest suites are installed under `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime`.

### Prompt

- Prompt: `Check that the deep-review divergent pivot refuses to run while a frontier node is open, seeds one revisit direction for each closed sub-target the frontier left uncovered, and leaves a default run untouched. Return a PASS or FAIL verdict with the reason.`

### Commands

1. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run divergent-pivot.vitest -t "review divergent pivot under the expansive depth mode" --reporter=verbose`
2. `cd .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime && npx vitest run stop-decision-yaml-contract.vitest -t "gates the divergent pivot on a terminal review frontier" --reporter=verbose`
3. `rg -n "frontier-unanswered" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/scripts/divergent-review-pivot.ts`

### Expected

Command 1 prints `Tests  3 passed | 23 skipped (26)`. Its three passing cases are `refuses a pivot while an open frontier node remains`, `seeds one frontier-unanswered direction per closed sub-target the frontier left uncovered` and `leaves a default run untouched even when the registry still carries an open frontier node`. Command 2 prints `Tests  1 passed | 33 skipped (34)`, and the case `gates the divergent pivot on a terminal review frontier` passes. Command 3 prints one line, 311, which sets the seed source to `frontier-unanswered`.

# Depth mode flag (--depth-mode)

## 1. OVERVIEW

`--depth-mode` is a command-level option on `/deep:research` and `/deep:review`. It takes `default` or `expansive`. The flag is not part of `mode-registry.json`, so the hub classifies a request into the same workflow mode with or without it. The behavior the flag turns on lives in the research and review packets.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Hub Classification Unchanged

`mode-registry.json` carries the `workflowMode`, `runtimeLoopType` and `backendKind` discriminator for `research` and `review`, and it has no depth field. Routing resolves the same packet whether the flag is present or not.

### Flag Routing

The command workflow reads `--depth-mode` and binds it as `depth_mode`. The runtime acts only on the value `expansive`. `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` adds `depthMode` to its stop-decision config only when the value is `expansive`, and `runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` passes `depthMode` to fan-out lineages only when the value is `expansive`. A default run carries no depth key in either place.

### Packet Behavior

The expansive behavior is documented in the packet catalogs: [expansive depth mode for research](../../deep-research/feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md) and [expansive depth mode for review](../../deep-review/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md).

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/mode-registry.json` | Registry | Holds the discriminator for `research` and `review`, with no depth field. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/research.md` | Command doc | The `--depth-mode` flag row for research. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/review.md` | Command doc | The `--depth-mode` flag row for review. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` | Script | Adds `depthMode` to the stop-decision config only when expansive. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` | Script | Passes `depthMode` to lineages only when expansive. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md` | Manual playbook | DR-068: the flag defaults to `default` and a default run matches the baseline. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md` | Manual playbook | DRV-076: the review flag defaults to `default` and a default run matches the baseline. |

---

### Depth mode flag (--depth-mode)

#### Description

The --depth-mode flag on /deep:research and /deep:review selects default or expansive depth, and leaves the registry-driven workflowMode, runtimeLoopType and backendKind classification unchanged.

#### How It Works

`mode-registry.json` has no depth field, and the runtime acts only on the expansive value. The behavior is documented in the research and review catalogs.

#### Source Files

See [`deep-loop-mode-classification/depth-mode-flag.md`](deep-loop-mode-classification/depth-mode-flag.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

---

### Completed-run extension flags (--extend, --extend-minutes)

#### Description

The --extend=N and --extend-minutes=M flags on /deep:research and /deep:review reopen a completed single-executor run in place, and leave the registry-driven workflowMode, runtimeLoopType and backendKind classification unchanged.

#### How It Works

`mode-registry.json` has no extension field. The extension sets `maxIterations` to the iterations run so far plus N, keeps the session and generation, and is refused with fan-out. The behavior is documented in the research and review catalogs.

#### Source Files

# system-deep-loop: Feature Catalog

This catalog inventories the live `system-deep-loop` hub surface. The hub classifies a request into one of two `workflowMode` packets (`research`, `review`) through a three-tier discriminator, holding no per-mode logic itself. A default-on, flag-gated compiled-routing fast path can resolve the same decision ahead of this registry-driven classification without changing what it resolves to.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

Use this catalog as the current-state inventory for the `system-deep-loop` hub. The hub classifies intent and loads the resolved packet; the `/deep:*` commands and native agent types are complementary surfaces that reach the same packets through their own static routers.

---

## 2. DEEP-LOOP MODE ROUTING

### Registry-Driven Deep-Loop Mode Classification

#### Description

`mode-registry.json` is the single source of truth for the hub's two `workflowMode` packets, each carrying a `workflowMode`/`runtimeLoopType`/`backendKind` three-tier discriminator.

#### Current Reality

`runtimeLoopType` is the graph-backed convergence key consumed by `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs`; `backendKind` is `runtime-loop-type` (research/review). A second-layer surface router at the root `ROUTER.md` supports router-replay benchmarking without re-deciding the mode.

#### Source Files

See [`deep-loop-mode-classification/deep-loop-mode-classification.md`](deep-loop-mode-classification/deep-loop-mode-classification.md) for the full discriminator and source anchors.

| Signal | Weight | Description |
|--------|--------|-------------|
| Rolling Average | 0.30 | Last 2 severity-weighted `newFindingsRatio` values average below `0.08` |
| MAD Noise Floor | 0.25 | Latest ratio within noise floor |
| Dimension Coverage | 0.45 | Every configured dimension (the four by default) plus required traceability protocols covered, with `minStabilizationPasses >= 1` |

**Key defaults:** `maxIterations=7`, `convergenceThreshold=0.10`, `rollingStopThreshold=0.08`, `noProgressThreshold=0.05`, `stuckThreshold=2`, `minIterations=2` (review floor, `antiConvergence.minIterations`), `minStabilizationPasses=1`

**P0 override:** Any new P0 finding sets `newFindingsRatio >= 0.50`, blocking convergence.

**Frontier veto:** Under `--depth-mode=expansive` an open or active frontier node turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the review floor.

---

## 9. AGENT ITERATION CHECKLIST

Each @deep-review iteration:
1. Read `deep-review-state.jsonl`, `deep-review-findings-registry.json`, and `deep-review-strategy.md`
2. Determine focus dimension from strategy "Next Focus"
3. Write `review/iterations/iteration-NNN.md` with P0/P1/P2 findings
4. Run adversarial self-check on any P0 findings (Hunter/Skeptic/Referee)

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '78,100p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/state/state-format.md
### Frontier Node Store (expansive depth only)

`research/frontier-nodes.jsonl` is the node-status store for a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive`. A default run never creates it. Rows are append-only and carry `{nodeId, parentId, depth, scope, question, status, reason, iteration, dedupeKey}`. The last row for a node wins, so a status change is one more row rather than an edit. `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs` is the only writer.

Statuses are `open`, `active`, `answered`, `blocked`, `exhausted`, `abstained` and `pruned`. A frontier question resolves when its node reaches a terminal status.

The reducer projects the store as `registry.frontier {nodes, stats, budget}`, applies the depth budget so an unspent share rolls down to the next depth only, closes a spent band's open nodes as `exhausted` with reason `budget`, and adds a `## 6B. FRONTIER` dashboard section.

---

## 3. OWNERSHIP MODEL

| Owner | Writes |
|-------|--------|
| Agent iteration | `iterations/iteration-NNN.md`, JSONL iteration/event append, optional progressive synthesis contribution |
| Workflow reducer | strategy machine-owned sections, findings registry, dashboard |
| Workflow synthesis | `research.md`, lifecycle snapshots |
| Spec anchoring protocol | bounded `spec.md` seed/context/fenced findings block |

The reducer is the source of truth for derived state. Manual edits to reducer-owned outputs are overwritten on the next refresh.

---

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '2480,2540p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs
.filter((node) => node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active');
  if (open.length === 0) return '';
  // Breadth first: the shallowest node, then one not yet visited, then the oldest.
  const ranked = open
    .map((node) => ({ node, visits: frontierNodeVisits(node.nodeId, iterationRecords) }))
    .sort((left, right) => (left.node.depth - right.node.depth)
      || (left.visits - right.visits)
      || ((left.node.iteration ?? 0) - (right.node.iteration ?? 0))
      || left.node.nodeId.localeCompare(right.node.nodeId));
  const chosen = ranked[0].node;
  const scope = Array.isArray(chosen.scope) ? chosen.scope.join(', ') : (chosen.scope || 'unscoped');
  return `Frontier node ${chosen.nodeId} (depth ${chosen.depth}, scope ${scope}): ${chosen.question}`;
}

// Depth bands share maxIterations 50/35/15 by default (depths 0-1, 2, 3). A band's
// unspent share rolls down to the next band only once the band has no open node,
// and never up. When a band's spend reaches its share, its open nodes close as
// exhausted with reason budget, so the run stops waiting on work it cannot fund.
// An open node whose question an iteration already answered is marked answered
// first: the budget closes only work still outstanding, and an answered node
// never holds its band open against the roll-down.
function applyFrontierBudget(nodes, iterationRecords, maxIterations, budgetSplit, answeredNodeIds = new Set()) {
  const split = budgetSplit && typeof budgetSplit === 'object' ? budgetSplit : {};
  const shares = [
    Number.isFinite(split.depth1) ? split.depth1 : 0.5,
    Number.isFinite(split.depth2) ? split.depth2 : 0.35,
    Number.isFinite(split.depth3) ? split.depth3 : 0.15,
  ];
  const cap = Number.isFinite(maxIterations) && maxIterations > 0 ? maxIterations : 10;
  const bandOf = (depth) => (depth <= 1 ? 0 : depth === 2 ? 1 : 2);
  const spent = [0, 0, 0];
  for (const node of nodes) {
    const visits = frontierNodeVisits(node.nodeId, (Array.isArray(iterationRecords) ? iterationRecords : [])
      .filter((record) => !['error', 'failed', 'failure', 'timeout'].includes(String(record?.status || '').toLowerCase())));
    spent[bandOf(node.depth)] += visits;
  }
  let result = nodes.map((node) => ((node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active') && answeredNodeIds.has(node.nodeId)
    ? { ...node, status: 'answered', reason: null }
    : { ...node }));
  const budget = {};
  let carry = 0;
  for (let band = 0; band < 3; band += 1) {
    const allotted = cap * shares[band] + carry;
    if (spent[band] >= allotted) {
      result = result.map((node) => (bandOf(node.depth) === band && (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active')
        ? { ...node, status: 'exhausted', reason: 'budget' }
        : node));
    }
    const bandOpen = result.some((node) => bandOf(node.depth) === band && (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active'));
    carry = bandOpen ? 0 : Math.max(0, allotted - spent[band]);
    budget[`depth${band + 1}`] = { allotted: Math.round(allotted * 100) / 100, spent: spent[band] };
  }
  return { nodes: result, budget };
}

  const latestBlockedStop = Array.isArray(registry.blockedStopHistory)
    ? registry.blockedStopHistory.at(-1)
    : null;
  const latestIteration = Array.isArray(iterationRecords) ? iterationRecords.at(-1) : null;
